## Commits to review

Adapted commits: differences between the official patch and the backport in files that go into the binaries (hunk line numbers ignored). Commits without an official counterpart: their whole patch.

-+static int is_userinfo_valid(zend_string *str)

 +{
 +	const char *valid = "-._~!$&'()*+,;=:";

-+	while (p - ZSTR_VAL(str) < ZSTR_LEN(str)) {
++	const char *p = str;

++
++	while (p - str < len) {
 +		if (isalpha(*p) || isdigit(*p) || strchr(valid, *p)) {
 +			p++;
-+		} else if (*p == '%' && p - ZSTR_VAL(str) <= ZSTR_LEN(str) - 3 && isdigit(*(p+1)) && isxdigit(*(p+2))) {
++		} else if (*p == '%' && p - str <= len - 3 && isdigit(*(p+1)) && isxdigit(*(p+2))) {
 +			p += 3;
--- official/ext/standard/url.c
+++ backport/ext/standard/url.c
@@ -2,12 +2,35 @@
 +++ b/ext/standard/url.c
-@@ PHPAPI php_url *php_url_parse_ex2(char const *str, size_t length, zend_bool *has
- 			ret->pass = zend_string_init(pp, (p-pp), 0);
- 			php_replace_controlchars_ex(ZSTR_VAL(ret->pass), ZSTR_LEN(ret->pass));
+@@ PHPAPI php_url *php_url_parse(char const *str)
+ 	return php_url_parse_ex(str, strlen(str));
+ }
+ 
+-static int is_userinfo_valid(const char *str, size_t len)
+-{
+-	char *valid = "-._~!$&'()*+,;=:";
+-	char *p = str;
+-	while (p - str < len) {
+-		if (isalpha(*p) || isdigit(*p) || strchr(valid, *p)) {
+-			p++;
+-		} else if (*p == '%' && p - str <= len - 3 && isdigit(*(p+1)) && isxdigit(*(p+2))) {
+-			p += 3;
+-		} else {
+-			return 0;
+-		}
+-	}
+-	return 1;
+-}
+-
+ /* {{{ php_url_parse
+  */
+ PHPAPI php_url *php_url_parse_ex(char const *str, size_t length)
+@@ PHPAPI php_url *php_url_parse_ex(char const *str, size_t length)
+ 			ret->pass = estrndup(pp, (p-pp));
+ 			php_replace_controlchars_ex(ret->pass, (p-pp));
  		} else {
--            if (!is_userinfo_valid(s, p-s)) {
--                goto check_port;
--            }
--            ret->user = zend_string_init(s, (p-s), 0);
-+			ret->user = zend_string_init(s, (p-s), 0);
- 			php_replace_controlchars_ex(ZSTR_VAL(ret->user), ZSTR_LEN(ret->user));
+-			if (!is_userinfo_valid(s, p-s)) {
+-				goto check_port;
+-			}
+ 			ret->user = estrndup(s, (p-s));
+ 			php_replace_controlchars_ex(ret->user, (p-s));
+-
  		}

<details><summary>#60 669f852 Fix GHSA-3qgc-jrrr-25jv</summary>

```diff
+++ backport/sapi/cgi/cgi_main.c
--- a/sapi/cgi/cgi_main.c
+++ b/sapi/cgi/cgi_main.c
@@ int main(int argc, char *argv[])
 		}
 	}
 
+	/* Apache CGI will pass the query string to the command line if it doesn't contain a '='.
+	 * This can create an issue where a malicious request can pass command line arguments to
+	 * the executable. Ideally we skip argument parsing when we're in cgi or fastcgi mode,
+	 * but that breaks PHP scripts on Linux with a hashbang: `#!/php-cgi -d option=value`.
+	 * Therefore, this code only prevents passing arguments if the query string starts with a '-'.
+	 * Similarly, scripts spawned in subprocesses on Windows may have the same issue. */
 	if((query_string = getenv("QUERY_STRING")) != NULL && strchr(query_string, '=') == NULL) {
-		/* we've got query string that has no = - apache CGI will pass it to command line */
 		unsigned char *p;
 		decoded_query_string = strdup(query_string);
 		php_url_decode(decoded_query_string, strlen(decoded_query_string));
@@ int main(int argc, char *argv[])
 		if(*p == '-') {
 			skip_getopt = 1;
 		}
+
+		/* On Windows we have to take into account the "best fit" mapping behaviour. */
+#ifdef PHP_WIN32
+		if (*p >= 0x80) {
+			wchar_t wide_buf[1];
+			wide_buf[0] = *p;
+			char char_buf[4];
+			size_t wide_buf_len = sizeof(wide_buf) / sizeof(wide_buf[0]);
+			size_t char_buf_len = sizeof(char_buf) / sizeof(char_buf[0]);
+			if (WideCharToMultiByte(CP_ACP, 0, wide_buf, wide_buf_len, char_buf, char_buf_len, NULL, NULL) == 0
+				|| char_buf[0] == '-') {
+				skip_getopt = 1;
+			}
+		}
+#endif
+
 		free(decoded_query_string);
 	}
 
+++ backport/sapi/cgi/tests/ghsa-3qgc-jrrr-25jv.phpt
new file mode 100644
--- /dev/null
+++ b/sapi/cgi/tests/ghsa-3qgc-jrrr-25jv.phpt
@@
+--TEST--
+GHSA-3qgc-jrrr-25jv
+--SKIPIF--
+<?php
+include 'skipif.inc';
+if (PHP_OS_FAMILY !== "Windows") die("skip Only for Windows");
+
+$codepage = trim(shell_exec("powershell Get-ItemPropertyValue HKLM:\\SYSTEM\\CurrentControlSet\\Control\\Nls\\CodePage ACP"));
+if ($codepage !== '932' && $codepage !== '936' && $codepage !== '950') die("skip Wrong codepage");
+?>
+--FILE--
+<?php
+include 'include.inc';
+
+$filename = __DIR__."/GHSA-3qgc-jrrr-25jv_tmp.php";
+$script = '<?php echo "hello "; echo "world"; ?>';
+file_put_contents($filename, $script);
+
+$php = get_cgi_path();
+reset_env_vars();
+
+putenv("SERVER_NAME=Test");
+putenv("SCRIPT_FILENAME=$filename");
+putenv("QUERY_STRING=%ads");
+putenv("REDIRECT_STATUS=1");
+
+passthru("$php -s");
+
+?>
+--CLEAN--
+<?php
+@unlink(__DIR__."/GHSA-3qgc-jrrr-25jv_tmp.php");
+?>
+--EXPECTF--
+X-Powered-By: PHP/%s
+Content-type: %s
+
+hello world
```

-+static size_t scale_by_percent(size_t value, zend_long percent)
-+{
-+	if (percent <= 0) {
-+		return 0;
-+	}
-+
-+	size_t p = (size_t)percent;
-+	size_t q = value / 100;
-+	size_t r = value % 100;
-+
-+	if (q > SIZE_MAX / p) {
-+		return SIZE_MAX;
-+	}
-+
-+	size_t hi = q * p;
-+	size_t lo = r * (p / 100) + r * (p % 100) / 100;
-+
-+	if (hi > SIZE_MAX - lo) {
-+		return SIZE_MAX;
-+	}
-+	return hi + lo;
-+}

-+ZEND_API void ZEND_FASTCALL zend_handle_delayed_oom(void)
-+{

-+	zend_execute_data *execute_data = EG(current_execute_data);

-+	if (info->state != ZEND_OOM_STATE_PENDING) {
-+		return;
-+	}

-+	AG(mm_heap)->overflow = 1;
-+

-+	 * may expose it to userland. */

-+	info->message = message;
-+
-+	if (execute_data) {
-+		/* An exception may already be in flight, and zend_throw_unwind_exit()
-+		 * does not allow that */
-+		EG(exception) = NULL;
-+
-+		/* Object allocation should succeed unless it trips the hard limit */
-+		zend_throw_unwind_exit();
-+	}

-+	zend_try {
-+		zend_error_zstr_at(E_ERROR, info->filename, info->lineno, info->message);
-+	} zend_catch {
-+	} zend_end_try();
-+
-+	AG(mm_heap)->overflow = 0;
-+
-+	/* Bailout discards EG(current_execute_data). Would be better to avoid the bailout here. */
-+	EG(current_execute_data) = execute_data;

-+	/* This function may be called from the VM interrupt handler: opline is not

-+	EG(opline_before_exception) = NULL;

-+	zend_mm_oom_info_dtor(info);
-+	info->state = ZEND_OOM_STATE_HANDLED;
-+
-+	/* No userland frame to unwind: there is nothing to restore afterwards

-+	 * abort the request, the way an immediate OOM does. */
-+	if (!execute_data) {
-+		zend_bailout();
-+	}

+ 	size_t ret = zend_safe_address(nmemb, size, offset, &overflow);
+

+-		zend_error_noreturn(E_ERROR, "Possible integer overflow in memory allocation (%zu * %zu + %zu)", nmemb, size, offset);

+ 		return 0;

+-		zend_error_noreturn(E_ERROR, "Possible integer overflow in %s (%zu * %zu + %zu)", message, nmemb, size, offset);

+ 		return 0;
+ 	}
+ 	return ret;

--uint32_t zend_accel_get_class_name_map_ptr(zend_string *type_name)
-+/* We delay the publishing of CE cache slots until after ZCSG(map_ptr_last) is
-+ * published.
-+ * CE cache slots stored in interned strings are immediately visible to other
-+ * processes, so if we fail to update ZCSG(map_ptr_last) after publishing a
-+ * slot (due to a bailout/exit), that slot may be re-allocated for something
-+ * else by other processes. */
-+void zend_accel_pending_ce_cache_begin(void)
-+{
-+	ZEND_ASSERT(ZCG(locked));
-+
-+	/* A persist window left through a bailout caught by the caller (e.g.
-+	 * opcache_compile_file()) never published its slots. Drop them. */
-+	zend_accel_pending_ce_cache_discard();
-+
-+	ZCG(pending_ce_cache) = emalloc(sizeof(HashTable));
-+	zend_hash_init(ZCG(pending_ce_cache), 8, NULL, NULL, 0);
-+}
-+
-+void zend_accel_pending_ce_cache_commit(void)
-+{
-+	ZEND_ASSERT(ZCG(pending_ce_cache));
-+	/* Every pending slot must be covered by ZCSG(map_ptr_last) */
-+	ZEND_ASSERT(ZCSG(map_ptr_last) == CG(map_ptr_last));
-+
-+	ZEND_HASH_FOREACH_NUM_KEY_VAL(ZCG(pending_ce_cache), zend_ulong h, zval *zv) {
-+		zend_string *type_name = (zend_string*)(uintptr_t)h;
-+
-+		ZEND_ASSERT(!ZSTR_HAS_CE_CACHE(type_name));
-+		GC_SET_REFCOUNT(type_name, (uint32_t)Z_LVAL_P(zv));
-+		GC_ADD_FLAGS(type_name, IS_STR_CLASS_NAME_MAP_PTR);
-+	} ZEND_HASH_FOREACH_END();
-+
-+	zend_accel_pending_ce_cache_discard();
-+}
-+
-+void zend_accel_pending_ce_cache_discard(void)
-+{
-+	if (ZCG(pending_ce_cache)) {
-+		zend_hash_destroy(ZCG(pending_ce_cache));
-+		efree(ZCG(pending_ce_cache));
-+		ZCG(pending_ce_cache) = NULL;
-+	}
-+}
-+
-+void zend_accel_get_class_name_map_ptr(zend_string *type_name)
- {
- 	uint32_t ret;
-+	zval *slot;
- 
- 	if (zend_string_equals_literal_ci(type_name, "self") ||
- 			zend_string_equals_literal_ci(type_name, "parent")) {
--		return 0;
-+		return;
- 	}
- 
- 	/* We use type.name.gc.refcount to keep map_ptr of corresponding type */
- 	if (ZSTR_HAS_CE_CACHE(type_name)) {
--		return GC_REFCOUNT(type_name);
-+		return;
- 	}
- 
- 	if ((GC_FLAGS(type_name) & GC_IMMUTABLE)
- 	 && (GC_FLAGS(type_name) & IS_STR_PERMANENT)) {
-+		ZEND_ASSERT(ZCG(pending_ce_cache) && "Must be called inside a persist window");
-+		slot = zend_hash_index_lookup(ZCG(pending_ce_cache), (zend_ulong)(uintptr_t)type_name);
-+		if (Z_TYPE_P(slot) != IS_NULL) {
-+			/* Already allocated earlier in this window */
-+			return;
-+		}
- 		do {
- 			ret = ZEND_MAP_PTR_NEW_OFFSET();
- 		} while (ret <= 2);
--		GC_SET_REFCOUNT(type_name, ret);
--		GC_ADD_FLAGS(type_name, IS_STR_CLASS_NAME_MAP_PTR);
--		return ret;
-+		ZVAL_LONG(slot, ret);
- 	}
--
--	return 0;
- }

-+/* Give the POST handler a fresh $_POST or $_FILES to fill in. A request parses

-+	array_init(zv);

-+	php_stream_from_zval(stream, stream_zv);

-+	/* Tear down what a previous call set up, as the request shutdown would. */

-+		destroy_uploaded_files_hash();
-+	}

-+	php_stream *old_stream = zend_test_post_stream;

-+	zend_test_post_stream = stream;

-+	SG(request_info).request_body = NULL;
-+	SG(read_post_bytes) = 0;
-+	SG(post_read) = 0;

-+	bool bailed_out = false;
-+
-+	zend_try {
-+		/* Pick the post handler for the content type, read the body, then run

-+		efree(SG(request_info).content_type_dup);
-+	}

-+	zend_test_post_stream = old_stream;

<details><summary>#94 75342f1 missing in previous Avoid state corruption via bailout</summary>

```diff
+++ backport/Zend/zend_execute_API.c
--- a/Zend/zend_execute_API.c
+++ b/Zend/zend_execute_API.c
@@ void shutdown_executor(void) /* {{{ */
 		zend_close_rsrc_list(&EG(regular_list));
 	} zend_end_try();
 
+	if (fast_shutdown) {
+		/* may be used by zend_error before init_executor */
+		ZVAL_UNDEF(&EG(user_error_handler));
+		ZVAL_UNDEF(&EG(user_exception_handler));
+		memset(&EG(user_error_handlers_error_reporting), 0, sizeof(EG(user_error_handlers_error_reporting)));
+		memset(&EG(user_error_handlers), 0, sizeof(EG(user_error_handlers)));
+		memset(&EG(user_exception_handlers), 0, sizeof(EG(user_exception_handlers)));
+	}
+
 	zend_objects_store_free_object_storage(&EG(objects_store), fast_shutdown);
 
+	/* may be used by zend_error before init_executor */
+	EG(error_handling) = EH_NORMAL;
+	EG(exception) = NULL;
+
 	/* All resources and objects are destroyed. */
 	/* No PHP callback functions may be called after this point. */
 	EG(active) = 0;
```

</details>
